Important Note
Where manufacturer’s name and / or numbers are mentioned, they are purely for reference purposes only and not to indicate source of manufacture or any
connection in the course of trade with the manufacturer’s name.
Every effort has been made to ensure accuracy and compatibility, but there is a responsibility between seller and purchaser to be satisfied that the product
is wholly appropriate for the purpose of its intended use when reasonably used. If in any doubt for fitment and maintenance information, please refer to the
vehicle manufacturers recommendations.
Property in and title to goods supplied by the seller shall not pass to the purchaser until the price has been paid in full but any risk in respect of such goods
shall pass to the purchaser upon the shipment of the goods ex Bike Alert’s warehouse.

Very Important: Please note that the chain shown in this manual is for size reference only.
For the correct chain type and grade, please refer to the vehicle’s manufacturer manual.
Reasonable care has been exercised regarding the tabulation of this sprocket selector manual. All details contained in this selector manual are subject to
change without notice. No liability will be accepted for any omissions or errors in this manual.

Warranty
All sprockets manufactured by JT Sprockets® are guaranteed to be free of defects in material and workmanship. JT Sprockets will repair or replace free of
charge any pieces we recognise to be faulty. Said goods have to be returned free to our warehouse, packing and duties included. JT Sprockets makes no other
warranty of any kind, whether expressed or implied and do not take any responsibility upon eventual damages involved in the use of their products, even if
defective.

## Pagina 5

RACELITE ALUMINIUM 7075 T6



 JT Sprockets is proud to introduce the new RaceLite range of anodised
 7075-T6 aluminium competition sprockets. These new JT Racing sprockets
 are designed and engineered to withstand the extreme pro-race conditions,
 providing maximum strength and durability at minimum weight.

 Precision CNC machined to JT’s uncompromising standards from certified
 7075-T6 Ergal aviation grade aluminium alloy, the new RaceLite sprockets
 will be available in a hard wearing anodised black, orange, red or blue
 finish to compliment the colour scheme on all the major manufacturers’
 motocross and enduro bikes.

 The RaceLite range of competition sprockets, in addition to the full off
 road range, also includes fitments for road sport bikes. Made of the same
 hard wearing 7075 T6 Ergal, these rear sprockets have a rich black or
 gold anodised finish for the most popular Supersport and Superbike road
 race machines, with a full selection of gearing options available for those
 demanding track days.
